{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A philosophical problem whereby a man intends to walk a mile from a particular point, but an infinity of gods separately plan to obstruct him. One of them will raise a barrier to stop his further advance if he reaches the half-mile point, a second if he reaches the quarter-mile point, a third if he goes one-eighth of a mile, and so on ad infinitum. The paradox involves the fact that (i) the man cannot begin walking, because a barrier will stop him even at the shortest distance, but (ii) if he does not begin walking, no barrier will rise."],"topics":["human-sciences","philosophy","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":8716001,"upstream_offset":21039627187,"upstream_record_sha256":"a7b0f766491b2e1ae052978d13abc9ae1d0baf7f2004b4f839ef0f908ee72228","word":"Benardete's paradox"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"paddocks","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A small enclosure or field of grassland, especially one used to exercise or graze horses or other animals."],"tags":["also","figuratively"]},{"glosses":["An enclosure next to a racecourse where horses are paraded and mounted before a race and unsaddled after a race."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["hobbies","horse-racing","horseracing","horses","lifestyle","pets","racing","sports"]},{"glosses":["An area at a racing circuit where the racing vehicles are parked and worked on before and between races."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","motor-racing","racing","sports"]},{"glosses":["A field on which a game is played; a playing field."],"tags":["broadly","slang"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["A field of grassland of any size, either enclosed by fences or delimited by geographical boundaries, especially a large area for keeping cattle or sheep."],"tags":["Australia","New-Zealand","broadly"]},{"glosses":["A place in a superficial deposit where ore or washdirt (“earth rich enough in metal to pay for washing”) is excavated; also, a place for storing ore, washdirt, etc."],"tags":["Australia","New-Zealand","broadly"],"topics":["business","mining"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpædək/","tags":["Ireland","Received-Pronunciation","Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈpædək/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpæɾək]","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈpædək/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpædɨ̞k]","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpæɾɨ̞k]","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":392805,"upstream_offset":1879114870,"upstream_record_sha256":"19f5502e71b6cd3bd5d7453079d590665d90264008bc0d7f5d0c2ee27f562484","word":"paddock"}

{"forms":[{"form":"redshirts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A supporter of the Italian nationalist leader Giuseppe Garibaldi, especially a member of his thousand-man army which conquered Sicily."],"tags":["historical"]},{"glosses":["A revolutionary or anarchist."],"tags":["historical"]},{"glosses":["A college athlete who spends a year not participating in official athletic activities, but does not lose his or her eligibility to participate in following years."],"tags":["US"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Sensing danger, Captain Kirk decided to beam down to the surface with Spock, McCoy, and a couple of redshirts.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An unimportant character introduced only to be killed in order to underscore the peril to the important characters; an expendable character."],"tags":["slang"],"topics":["fiction","lifestyle","literature","media","publishing","science-fiction"]},{"glosses":["A person responsible for loading and unloading weapons, artillery, and equipment from aircraft."],"tags":["US"],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"Red Shirt"}],"glosses":["Alternative form of Red Shirt (“member of the UDD”)."],"tags":["alt-of","alternative"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹɛd.ʃəː(ɹ)t/","tags":["UK"]}],"upstream_line":490904,"upstream_offset":2294489690,"upstream_record_sha256":"e39316497db440aa16bdd1254b25f4cc1f37dc14f3a649d5ba82e2a9da14946f","word":"redshirt"}
